MEETING: City Council Meeting - Agenda Only | DATE: 2020-12-14 | CLIP_ID: 777 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 777

   
RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
December 14, 2020 
 
 The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:06 p.m. via electronic conference meeting .  Attending electronic conference meeting  were: 
MAYOR - Marian Orr, C OUNCIL MEMBERS – Rocky Case,  Ken Esquibel, Pete Laybourn, 
Mike Luna, Dr. Mark Rinne , Scott Roybal, Dicky Shanor and Jeff White.  Absent: Bryan Cook.  
Also attending: Kris Jones, City Clerk. [Clerk’s note: the pledge of allegiance was not recited due 
to the absence of a flag]   
 
 Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda. )  Mr. Luna moved to approve, seconded by Mr. White.  
Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present.  
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on November 23, 2020.  Motion 
(per Consent Agenda) carried. 
 
Mayor Orr opened a public hearing  to determine whether compliance of annexation 
conditions required by Wyoming Statute §15 -1-402 exist for land located southeast of the 
intersection of Powderhouse Road and Carlson Street.  Seth Lloyd, Planning and Development 
Department, advised the annex ation request was submitted by the landowner,  provided 
information on the property  and reviewed requirements of and compliance with annexation 
conditions contained in state statutes.   There being no further comments or input and purpose of 
the hearing having been met, Mayor Orr closed the hearing. 
 
ORDINANCE – 3rd READING – Amending Chapter 10.44, Weight Limits, of Title 10, 
Vehicles and Traffic, of the Municipal Code of the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming .  Reporting for 
Public Services Committee, Mr. Case moved to approve on third and final reading, seconded by 
Mr. Roybal. Upon inquiry related to if scales have been purchased,  Chief Brian Kozak, Police 
Department, explained the Police Department has some scales that Highway Patrol loaned them a 
few years ago that they may be able to keep.  Chief Kozak advised that if they are unable to keep 
the scales, the city would purchase scales with drug forfeiture funding.  Support was expressed to 
Chief Kozak, the trucking industry and City employees for their work involved with the ordinance.  
Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present with exception of Mr. 
Case, Mr. Luna and Mr. Shanor voting “no”. (#4333) 
 
ORDINANCE –3rd READING – Amending Section 2.84.070, Board of Adjustment, of 
Chapter 2.84, Miscellaneous Boards and Commissions, of Title 2, Administration and Personnel, 
of the Municipal Code of the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, to correct cross references to the Board 
   
of Adjustment.  Charles Bloom, Director of Planning and Development, advised the  ordinance 
corrects cross references to the Board of Adjustment in Title 17.  Mr. Case moved to approve on 
third and final reading, seconded by Mr . Luna.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of 
the governing body present. (#4334) 
 
ORDINANCE –3rd READING – Amending Section 15.52.060(B), Notification of 
Issuance or Denial -Appeal, and Section 15.52.100, Variances, of Chapter 15.52, Solar Access 
Permit System, of Title 15, Buildings and Construction, of the Municipal Code of the City of 
Cheyenne, Wyoming, to correct cross references to the Board of Adjustment . Charles Bloom , 
Director of Planning and Development, advised the ordinance corrects cross references to Title 17 
in Title 15.  Mr. Case moved to approve on third and final  reading, seconded by Mr . Roybal.  
Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present. (#4335) 
 
ORDINANCE –3rd READING – Establishing a Planned Unit Development (PUD) District 
for property generally located northwest of the intersection of North Gate Avenue and Yellowstone 
Road.  Seth Lloyd, Planning and Development Department, advised  the zone change request to 
PUD would allow for a  duplex development.  Mr. Case moved to approve on third and final  
reading, seconded by Mr . Luna.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” - all members of the governing 
body present. (#4336) 
 
ORDINANCE –1st READING – Annexing to the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, land 
located southeast of the intersection of Powderhouse Road and East Carlson Street – referred to 
Public Services Committee. (Sponsor – Mr. Case) 
 
ORDINANCE –1st READING – Amending the Official Zoning Map of th e City of 
Cheyenne changing the zoning classification from County A-1 Agricultural and Rural Residential 
to P Public District for a tract of land generally located southeast of the intersection of 
Powderhouse Road and East Carlson Street – referred to Public Services Committee.  (Sponsor – 
Mr. Case) 
 
ORDINANCE –1st READING – Amending the Unified Development Code (UDC) to 
define, establish permissibility, and create specific use standards for the use ‘Mini Storage’ – 
referred to Public Services Committee. (Sponsor – Mr. Case) 
 
ORDINANCE –1st READING – Amending Section 2.04.020, Mayor -Election-Term of 
Office, of Chapter 2.04, Mayor and City Council, of Title 2, Administration and Personnel, of the 
Municipal Code of the city of Cheyenne, Wyoming – referred to P ublic Services Committee. 
(Sponsor – Dr. Rinne)  
 
 RESOLUTION – To rededicate unknown funds and unused donations from various 
accounts within the City of Cheyenne’s Agency Fund .  Reporting for Finance Committee , Mr. 
Luna moved to adopt, seconded by Mr. Esquibel.  Mr. Luna moved to amend by substitute dated 
12/8/2020 which includes the amendment made at the 12/ 7/2020 Finance Committee meeting 
which removes the line item title “County Pockets/Urban Planning Office” in an amount of 
$23,278.08 to be rededicated to the “Loan Annexation Fund”, seconded by Mr. Esquibel.  Support 
   
was expressed for the substitute  as the funds will go towards development of county pockets.   
Motion to amend carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present.  Main motion 
as amended carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body present. (#6104)  
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Repealing a portion of Resolution No. 5951 which pertains to the 
dedication of General Fund Reserves to design and build a second level on the Cheyenne 
Municipal Court Building.  Motion (per Consent Agenda) carried. (#6105) 
 
 RESOLUTION – Certifying CONNECT 2045:  Update to the Master Transportation and 
Future Land Use Plan of PlanCheyenne for the City of Cheyenne and the Cheyenne Ur ban Area.  
Tom Mason, Director of MPO, advised the resolution is presenting the update known as Connect 
2045 and explained the plan has been in development for 1.5 years between city, county, WYDOT 
and other steering committee members.  He noted the County Commissioner’s have adopted the 
plan and the next step is city approval and MPO Policy Committee for final adoption and approval 
of the plan.  Mr. Mason provided information on the transportation plan noting it is updated every 
5 years and plan adoption allows federally funded transportation project s to move forward.  Mr. 
Mason advised recently a small list of technical corrections w as provided to be made to the final 
document.  Mayor Orr expressed appreciation to Allison Fluitt with Kimley Horn for her work on 
the project.  Mr. Case moved to adopt, seconded by Mr. Roybal.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – 
all members of the governing body present. (#6106)

MEETING: City Council Meeting - Agenda Only | DATE: 2020-12-28 | CLIP_ID: 779 | SOURCE: cheyenne.granicus.com MinutesViewer clip 779

   
RECORD OF PROCEEDINGS FOR THE GOVERNING BODY 
OF THE CITY OF CHEYENNE 
Office of City Clerk 
 
December 28, 2020 
 
 The Governing Body of the City of Cheyenne met in regular session on this date beginning 
at 6:06 p.m. via electronic conference meeting .  Attending electronic conference meeting  were: 
MAYOR - Marian Orr, COUNCIL MEMBERS – Rocky Case, Bryan Cook, Ken Esquibel, Pete 
Laybourn, Mike Luna,  Dr. Mark Rinne , Scott Roybal, Dicky Shanor and Jeff White.   Also 
attending: Kris Jones, City Clerk. The Pledge of Allegiance was recited. 
 
 Consent Agenda.  (All agenda items listed with the designation of [CA] are considered to 
be routine items by the governing body and will be enacted by one motion.  There will be no 
separate discussion on these items unless a member of the governing body so requests and support 
by two other members is received.  Any item removed from the Consent Agenda will be considered 
in its normal sequence on the agenda. )  Upon request by Dr. R inne, and pursuant to support 
received by Mr. Cook and Mr. Roybal, agenda item #13 was removed from the Consent Agenda.  
Mr. Luna moved to approve remaining items, seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried.  Voting 
“yes” – all members of the governing body.  
 
[CA] Minutes from Regular Meeting of the Governing Body on December 14, 2020.  Motion 
(per Consent Agenda) carried. 
 
ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Annexing to the City of Cheyenne, Wyoming, land 
located southeast of the intersection of Powderhouse Road and East Carlson Street .  Charles 
Bloom, Planning and Development Director, advised the annexation request is from Laramie 
County School District #1 for the purpose of constructing a new elementary school for grades 5-6 
and explained there is an affiliated item for a zone change request  and final plat on the agenda.    
Reporting for Public Services Committee, Mr. Case moved to approve on second reading,  
seconded by Mr. Luna.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body. 
 
ORDINANCE – 2nd READING – Amending the Official Zoning Map of the City of 
Cheyenne changing the zoning classification from County A-1 Agricultural and Rural Residential 
to P Public District for a tract of land generally located southeast of the intersection of 
Powderhouse Road and East Carlson Street.  Charles Bloom, Planning and Development Director, 
advised the zone change request is related to the previous annexation request from Laramie County 
School District #1 for the purpose of constructing a new elementary school for grades 5-6.  Mr. 
Case moved to approve on second reading, seconded by Mr. Luna.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” 
– all members of the governing body. 
 
ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending the Unified Development Code (UDC) to 
define, establish permissibility, and create specific use standards for the use ‘Mini Storage’.  Mark 
Christensen, Planning and Development  Department, explained the amendment stems from a 
specific request and updates storage-personal and storage-park and combines them as a cohesive 
   
definition.  Mr. Christensen advised that if a storage unit park is located in certain zoned areas, 
they must meet  certain criteria and noted that staff recommends approval.   Mr. Case moved to 
approve on second reading, seconded by Mr. White.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” – all members 
of the governing body. 
 
ORDINANCE –2nd READING – Amending Section 2.04.020, Mayor -Election-Term of 
Office, of Chapter 2.04, Mayor and City Council, of Title 2, Administration and Personnel, of the 
Municipal Code of the city of Ch eyenne, Wyoming .  Dr. Rinne explained the ordinance 
amendment changes the Mayor’s office employees from regular employees to “at will” so future 
mayors can choose their own staff members.   Dr. Rinne clarified this change does not include the 
current staff members in the mayor’s office  and that the change s is intended for the future.  Mr. 
Case moved to approve on second reading, seconded by Mr. Cook.  Motion carried.  Voting “yes” 
– all members of the governing body. 
 
 RESOLUTION – Designating local banks as depositories for municipal funds . Brenda 
Moreaux, Deputy City Treasurer, provided information on the resolution stating statutes require 
municipalities designate local banks  as depositories for municipal funds.  She explained staff 
supports approval of a proposed substitute resolution which adds Wells Fargo Bank to the listing.  
Reporting for Finance Committee, Mr. Luna moved to adopt, seconded by Mr.  White.  Mr. Luna 
moved to amend by substitute dated December 16, 2020, seconded by Mr.  Cook.  Ms. Moreaux 
advised all banks were given a deadline  to respond and Wells Fargo had a late response so the 
amendment now includes that bank.  Motion to amend carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the 
governing body.  Main motion as amended carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing 
body. (#6108) 
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Reappropriating the Fiscal Year 2021 Budget of the City of Cheyenne 
according to the provisions of W.S. §16 -4-112 & §16 -4-113.  Motion (per Consent Agenda) 
carried. (#6109) 
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Supporting the City of Cheyenne’s application for a Land and Water 
Conservation Fund Grant to replace playground safety surfacing (revenue to the city).  Motion (per 
Consent Agenda) carried.  (#6110) 
 
 RESOLUTION – Authorizing the Mayor and the City Clerk to sign a Final Plat for Capital 
Heights Addition, Third Filing, a replat of all of Lots 3 and 4, Block 1018, Capital Heights 
Addition, Cheyenne, Laramie County, Wyoming (located west of and adjacent to Dillon Avenue 
north of Foyer Avenue, currently addressed as 3810 Dillon Avenue).  Connor White, Planning and 
Development Department, provided information on the property including location and noted the 
final plat creates 4 lots for development of 4 townhomes .  Mr. White reported both the Planning 
Commission and city staff recommend approval.  Mr. Case moved to adopt, seconded by Mr. 
White.  Comment was made in opposition of the resolution and how the proposal isn’t a good fit 
for an established neighborhood as this type of development could impact property value.  Further 
comments were made acknowledging that city staff is making recommendations in compliance 
with current city code regulations.  It was further noted that the proposed final plat meets existing 
city code regulations.   Upon inquiry, Mr. White confirmed the replat consists of dividing 2 lots 
   
into 4 individual lots and the town houses will be approximately 20 ft. wide.   Comments were 
made relating to other similar lots within existing neighborhoods that have not had any negative 
impact on value or esthetics.  Upon inquiry, members of Planning and Development staff and Ward 
I City Council members confirmed they have not received any negative communication or 
comments from area residents regarding the proposed development.  Additional comments in 
support of the proposed infill project were voiced.  Upon inquiry, Charles Bloom, Planning and 
Development Director, provided information on the notification process which is the responsibility 
of the contractor to provide to area residents.  Mr. Bloom advised the required notice shall be 
provided and sign age posted prior to the first P lanning Commission meeting.   Motion carried.   
Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body with the exception of Dr. Rinne voting “no”. 
(#6111) 
 
 RESOLUTION – Approving a Special Use Permit for oil and gas support services for Lot 
1, Block 1, Plains Industrial Park, 2 nd Filing, Laramie County, Wyoming .  Charles Bloom, 
Planning and Development Director, advised the city received a request to authorize a drilling 
operation to go along with oil extraction that was approved by the Board of Adjustment in 
December of 2019.  Mr. Bloom explained that Section 5.8.7 of the Unifie d Development Code 
allows the Governing Body to temporarily defer all or a portion of the development requirements 
in City Code and noted that the development will be temporary.  Mr. Bloom reported the proposed 
substitute includes the following deferral co ntributions: Deposits in the City’s Agency Fund for 
$29,389.59 in the Greenway Account, $5,962.50 in the Forestry account and $27,610.00 in the 
Park Enhancement Account which is park in lieu fees that are required at time of subdivision.  He 
further advised that staff recommends approval and that the exhibit referenced in the resolution is 
the last item in the pdf packet.  Mr. Case moved to adopt, seconded by Mr.  Roybal.  Mr. Case 
moved to amend by substitute dated December 17, 2020, seconded by Mr . Roybal.  Motion to 
amend carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body.  Main motion as amended 
carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body. (#6112) 
 
[CA] RESOLUTION – Authorizing the Mayor and City Clerk to sign a final plat for Coyote 
Ridge, a plat of a portion of the northwest quarter of Section 20, Cheyenne, Laramie County, 
Wyoming (located at the southeast corner of Powderhouse Road and East Carlson Street) – 
referred to Public Services Committee. (Sponsor – Rocky Case)

[CA] Contract between the City of Cheyenne and Peak Wellness, Alcohol Receiving Ce nter, to

 [CA] Agreement between the City of Cheyenne, Laramie County Sheriff’s Department and

[CA] Contract Modification #1 to Contract # 7241 between the City of Cheyenne and Mid-

[CA] Contract Modification #2 to Contract # 7207 between the City of Cheyenne and  Front

[CA] Contract Modification #2 to Contract #6927 between Wyoming Homeland Security and

[CA] Consideration of Bid #E-16-21 for furnishing three (3) new greens mowers, for the City of

 No comments were made on the Voucher Report as presented. 
 
 No comments were made on the Revenue Report as presented. 
 
   
 Appointments of Gus Lopez to the Cheyenne -Laramie County Board of Health; Wendy 
Soto and Erin LeBlanc to the Housing and Communit y Development Advisory Council; Dustin 
Brown, Richard Maxted, and Ben Moritz to the Board of Adjustment, and Michael Vigil to the 
Building Code Board of Appeals.  Mr. Luna moved to approve, seconded by Mr. Shanor.  Motion 
carried.  Voting “yes” – all members of the governing body with the exception of Mayor Orr who 
did not vote (mayoral appointments). 
 
 Announcement was made of  the Sine Die and First Regular Meetings of the Governing 
Body will be held Monday, January 4, 2021 beginning at 6:00 p.m. in the Cheyenne Civic Center, 
510 West 20th Street, Cheyenne, Wyoming and via electronic conference meeting. 
 
 Announcement was made that due to the Sine Die and First Regular Meetings of the 
Governing Body on Monday, January 4, 20 21, regularly scheduled Cit y Council Committee 
meetings will instead be held as follows: Finance Committee will meet at Noon on  Tuesday, 
January 5, 2021 via electronic conference meeting  and Public Services Committee will meet at 
Noon on Wednesday, January 6, 2021 via electronic conference meeting.  Additionally, all Finance 
Committee meetings for 2021 will be held at Noon instead of 11:00 a.m. 
 
 Announcement was made of a Public Hearing to be held January 11, 2021 at 6:00 p.m. via
